### 4103. [restrict](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/restrict) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈstrɪkt/` · **US:** `/rɪˈstrɪkt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/restr/restrict__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/restr/restrict__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** बाधित करना, सीमित करना, परिमित/सीमित करना
- **Examples:**
  - restrict something to something Speed is restricted to 30 mph in towns.
  - We restrict the number of students per class to ten.
  - restrict something Fog severely restricted visibility.
  - Having small children tends to restrict your freedom.

### 4104. [restriction](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/restriction) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈstrɪkʃn/` · **US:** `/rɪˈstrɪkʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/restr/restriction__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/restr/restriction__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** सीमा, रोक, प्रतिबन्ध
- **Examples:**
  - import/speed/travel restrictions
  - restriction on something to impose/place a restriction on something
  - The government has agreed to lift restrictions on press freedom.
  - There are no restrictions on the amount of money you can withdraw.

### 4105. [resume](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/resume_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈzjuːm/` · **US:** `/rɪˈzuːm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/resum/resume__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/resum/resume__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** फिर आरम्भ करना, पुनः प्राप्त करना, संक्षेप तैयार करना
- **Examples:**
  - resume (something) to resume talks/negotiations
  - She resumed her career after an interval of six years.
  - The noise resumed, louder than before.
  - There is no sign of the peace talks resuming.

### 4389. [speculate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/speculate) *(verb)*

- **Examples:**

  - speculate why, how, etc… It is useless to speculate why he did it.
  - speculate that… We can speculate that the stone circles were used in some sort of pagan ceremony.

### 4390. [speculation](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/speculation) *(noun)*

  - speculation over something The president’s absence led to speculation over his health.

### 4607. [testify](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/testify) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈtestɪfaɪ/` · **US:** `/ˈtestɪfaɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/tes/testi/testify__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/tes/testi/testify__us_1.mp3)

### 4608. [testimony](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/testimony) *(noun)*

- **Meaning:** प्रमाण, गवाही
- **Examples:**

  - The pyramids are an eloquent testimony to the ancient Egyptians' engineering skills.
  - His thick, swollen fingers bore testimony to a lifetime of toil.

## Words 4,801 – 4,900

### 4801. [uniform](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/uniform_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈjuːnɪfɔːm/` · **US:** `/ˈjuːnɪfɔːrm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/u/uni/unifo/uniform__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/u/uni/unifo/uniform__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** एक समान, एकसमान त्वरण, बराबर
- **Examples:**
  - The hat is part of the school uniform.
  - a military uniform
  - Do you have to wear uniform?
  - in (a) uniform He was dressed in the uniform of a Royal Navy officer.

### 4813. [unless](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/unless) *(conjunction)*
- **UK:** `/ənˈles/` · **US:** `/ənˈles/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/u/unl/unles/unless__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/u/unl/unles/unless__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** यदि नहीं/जो नहीं/बिना/जबतक नहीं
- **Examples:**
  - You won't get paid for time off unless you have a doctor's note.
  - I won't tell them—not unless you say I can.
  - Unless I'm mistaken, she was back at work yesterday.
  - He hasn't got any hobbies—unless you call watching TV a hobby.

### 4814. [unlike](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/unlike_1) *(preposition)*
- **UK:** `/ˌʌnˈlaɪk/` · **US:** `/ˌʌnˈlaɪk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/u/unl/unlik/unlike__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/u/unl/unlik/unlike__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** असमान, प्रतिकूल, के विपरीत
- **Examples:**
  - Music is quite unlike any other art form.
  - The sound was not unlike that of birds singing.

### 4815. [unlikely](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/unlikely) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ʌnˈlaɪkli/` · **US:** `/ʌnˈlaɪkli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/u/unl/unlik/unlikely__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/u/unl/unlik/unlikely__us_1.mp3)
- **Meaning:** अविश्वसनीय, असंभाव्य, असम्भव
- **Examples:**
  - unlikely to do something The project seemed unlikely to succeed.
  - Of course, such an event is very unlikely to happen.
  - it is unlikely (that…) It was highly unlikely that the gunshot wound was self-inflicted.
  - It is extremely unlikely that the new owner would want to make people redundant.
